Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [113] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST通过基于全局模型参数强度的自适应更新幅度调节和线性凸约束，解决了联邦学习中的过拟合和泛化问题，在不同FL环境中实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中全局模型泛化能力差，主要由于客户端数据异构导致局部模型过拟合本地分布，且可迁移特征在聚合过程中被扭曲。

Method: 提出FedCONST方法：1) 基于全局模型参数强度自适应调节更新幅度，避免过度强化已学好的参数，同时加强未充分学习的参数；2) 采用线性凸约束确保训练稳定性并保留局部学习的泛化能力；3) 利用梯度信噪比(GSNR)分析验证特征可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Result: FedCONST有效对齐了局部和全局目标，减轻过拟合，在不同异构FL环境中实现了最先进的性能。GSNR分析验证了其在增强特征可迁移性和鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FedCONST通过自适应调制更新幅度和线性凸约束，成功缓解了联邦学习中的数据异构带来的泛化问题，是一种有效的联邦学习优化方法。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [114] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对比了多种深度学习架构、零样本基础模型（FM）和统计基线在可穿戴设备行为时间序列（步数、屏幕使用时间、睡眠时长）上的多步预测性能，基于三个公开数据集（超800名参与者），并考察了跨人群泛化、参与者级微调以及多天预测精度衰减等问题。主要发现包括：PatchTST在训练模型中表现最佳，但前三名（TCN、MLP、Transformer）差异不显著；TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配甚至超越训练模型，尤其在低数据场景下；参与者级微调可降低RMSE 16-60%，睡眠改善最明显，步数最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和智能手机生成的丰富行为时间序列可用于主动健康干预，但目前缺乏对这些数据上现代预测架构的系统性比较，特别是在跨人群泛化、参与者级微调以及多天预测精度退化方面的研究尚属空白。

Method: 在三个公开数据集（超800参与者）上，对六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型及统计基线进行基准测试，报告了步数、屏幕使用时间和睡眠时长在1-8天预测窗口上的每个特征指标；进一步对所有六种架构进行每特征个性化研究，并评估基础模型在不同数据集大小和时间粒度上的迁移能力。

Result: (i) 无单一架构独占鳌头，PatchTST在训练模型中领先，但前三名（TCN, MLP, Transformer）无显著性能差异；(ii) TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型，尤其低数据场景；(iii) 参与者级微调使每特征RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠改善最大，步数最小。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动健康预测中的架构选择、基础模型适用性及个性化策略提供了实用指导，是首次联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型和个性化在可穿戴设备多步行为预测中的研究。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [115] [EM-NeSy: Expectation Maximization for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14463)
*Annegret Seibt,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EM-NeSy将概率神经符号学习建模为EM算法实例，通过期望步进行概率推理计算后验，最大化步仅通过神经组件进行梯度更新，实现了无需符号组件可微的近似推理，并在精确推理下恢复标准端到端梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的神经符号模型要求符号组件以可微方式表达，这常常使得近似推理的使用变得复杂，限制了NeSy模型的灵活性和适用范围。

Method: 将概率神经符号学习形式化为EM算法：E步通过概率推理计算在标签条件下神经预测符号的后验分布；M步基于该后验仅通过神经组件进行梯度下降更新参数。

Result: 实验结果表明EM-NeSy具有良好的可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法释放了EM算法在神经符号学习中的全部潜力，无需额外修改或符号组件的可微性要求即可自然扩展到近似推理，同时在精确推理下可恢复标准端到端梯度方法。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic (NeSy) models integrate neural networks and symbolic reasoning for robust and interpretable AI. State-of-the-art NeSy models require that the symbolic component is expressed in a differentiable way, often complicating the use of approximate inference. We propose EM-NeSy which casts probabilistic NeSy learning as an instance of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In the expectation step, we compute the posterior over the neurally predicted symbols conditioned on the label via probabilistic inference. In the maximization step, we update the neural parameters based on this posterior using gradient descent only through the neural component. This formulation unlocks the full potential of the EM algorithm for NeSy learning. It allows NeSy to extend naturally to approximate reasoning without any additional modifications or differentiability requirements of the symbolic component. Furthermore, it recovers the standard end-to-end gradient-based NeSy setting under exact inference. Our experimental results demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of EM-NeSy.

</details>


### [116] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合专家增强的自适应深度聚类生存框架（AdaCSM），通过路由式专家机制实现患者动态分配到专门的生存预测器，同时保留生存分析和亚型聚类目标，在多个真实临床队列中表现出优于现有方法的预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型通常为所有患者学习单一共同特征表示，忽略了患者亚组间的重要差异，无法捕捉异质性生存模式。

Method: 提出AdaCSM框架，采用混合专家（MoE）架构，引入基于路由的专家机制，在参数化生存建模框架内实现条件专业化，动态分配患者到专门的风险预测器，同时优化生存预测和亚型聚类目标。

Result: 在多个涵盖不同疾病领域的真实纵向临床队列上，与现有最先进的生存分析和深度聚类模型相比，AdaCSM在预测性能上表现更优，并产生可解释的生存分析结果。

Conclusion: AdaCSM通过MoE架构有效建模患者异质性生存模式，提升了预测准确性和结果可解释性，为临床风险分层和患者管理提供了更精细化的工具。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [117] [Recipe-Controlled Decoder Audit for Structural Knowledge-Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14492)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配方控制的解码器审计（RCDA）方法，用于结构化归纳知识图谱补全。通过在相同训练配方下交换解码器（如ComplEx和DistMult），在七个基准上评估编码器与解码器对性能的实际贡献，发现解码器效应在小型KG中尤为显著且对配方和来源敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱补全研究往往将性能提升笼统归因于编码器或训练配方，而忽略了解码器选择的影响。本文旨在通过受控实验分离解码器效应，提供更严谨的归因审计方法。

Method: 采用配方控制的解码器审计（RCDA），以ComplEx和DistMult作为主要受控对比对，辅以RotatE/TransE的针对性检查；在7个基准上固定配方仅交换解码器，报告MRR差异；对小型KG进行多种子实验和来源变体分析。

Result: 五个标准KG上ComplEx与DistMult差异较小（+0.005~+0.012 MRR），CompGCN编码器效应变化更大；小型KG上解码器效应为主：Kinship中ComplEx稳定领先+0.143 MRR，UMLS中反转取决于数据来源；解码器选择与编码器深度交互；L=0时ComplEx在YAGO3-10达到0.6971±0.0048 MRR。

Conclusion: 提出简洁审计协议：报告匹配解码器的行、记录小型KG来源、在做出编码器层面声明前进行解码器×深度的遍历。小型KG的解码器选择应被视为配方和来源敏感的，而非固定数据集获胜者。

Abstract: We present a recipe-controlled decoder audit (RCDA) for structural transductive knowledge-graph completion (KGC). The audit asks a simple reporting question: before attributing gains to an encoder or training recipe, what changes when the decoder is swapped under the same recipe? Using ComplEx and DistMult as the primary controlled pair, with targeted RotatE/TransE spot-checks, we evaluate seven benchmarks. On five standard KGs, ComplEx-vs-DistMult differences are modest but consistent under our recipe (+0.005 to +0.012 MRR), whereas CompGCN-style encoder effects vary more by dataset. On small KGs, decoder effects become the main diagnostic: Kinship shows a stable ComplEx advantage of +0.143 MRR (6 seeds), while UMLS favours ComplEx by +0.022 MRR in a clean 6-seed server rerun but reverses in an earlier provenance variant. We therefore treat small-KG decoder choice as recipe- and provenance-sensitive rather than as a fixed dataset winner. We further show that decoder choice interacts with encoder depth on WN18RR, and that under our recipe L=0 ComplEx on YAGO3-10 reaches 0.6971 +/- 0.0048 MRR at d=128. The result is a compact audit protocol: report matched decoder rows, log small-KG provenance, and sweep decoder x depth before making encoder-level claims.

</details>


### [118] [PepALD: Macrocyclic Peptide Generation via Autoregressive Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14510)
*Junming Zhang,Siyu Yi,Wei Ju,Zhonghui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PepALD是一个基于自回归潜扩散模型的基础模型，用于从头设计大环肽，通过结构化化学嵌入、上下文条件扩散、R基团感知环闭合和偏好优化，在生成质量和奖励优化方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大环肽是细胞内靶点的有前景的治疗候选物，但其设计需要同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜渗透性和靶标结合。现有基于SMILES或HELM字符串的生成模型要么在原子级序列空间操作，要么将单体视为化学基础有限的符号化token，缺乏有效的化学表征。

Method: PepALD采用自回归潜扩散（ALD）架构：使用结构化化学嵌入表示HELM单体，通过上下文条件扩散在化学信息潜空间中生成每个残基，在自回归生成过程中预测R基团感知的环闭合，并利用胜者保护扩散适配偏好优化将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。

Result: 在生成质量和奖励优化性能方面，PepALD优于代表性的大环肽生成基线方法。

Conclusion: PepALD通过融合结构化化学嵌入、潜扩散生成和偏好优化，有效解决大环肽设计中的多目标约束问题，为从头设计提供了新的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Macrocyclic peptides are promising therapeutic candidates for intracellular targets, but their design requires simultaneous control over non-natural monomer chemistry, ring topology, membrane permeability, and target binding. Existing SMILES- or HELM-string generative models either operate in long atom-level sequence spaces or treat monomers as symbolic tokens with limited chemical grounding. We introduce PepALD, an Autoregressive Latent Diffusion (ALD) foundation model for \textit{de novo} macrocyclic peptide generation. The model represents HELM monomers with structured chemical embeddings, generates each residue through context-conditioned diffusion in chemically informed latent space, predicts R-group-aware ring closures during autoregressive generation, and aligns the denoiser to affinity rewards using winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization. In silico experiments demonstrate PepALD's generation quality and reward-optimization performance against representative peptide generation baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Behavioral Audit of Machine Unlearning Has a Privacy Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14518)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信息论证明，在模型所有者不诚实且审计者诚实但好奇的互不信任场景下，仅依赖查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的情况下识别未充分学习的模型，揭示了机器学习未学习（MU）审计中固有的隐私-审计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习未学习（MU）研究缺乏统一的审计方案，已有工作显示不诚实的模型所有者可以伪造证据逃避执行MU，而好奇的审计者可以推断模型和训练数据的隐私敏感属性，但双方互不信任下的MU审计问题尚未被探索。

Method: 论文采用信息论方法对互不信任场景下的MU审计进行理论证明；对凸ML模型证明仅依赖行为信号的通用审计方案无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的前提下识别未充分学习模型；通过凸模型上的实验结果支持理论发现，并在非凸模型上进一步实验验证隐私-审计权衡的普遍性。

Result: 凸ML模型中，仅查询行为信号的审计方案固有地存在隐私-审计权衡，无法同时实现有效的审计和保留集成员隐私保护；实验结果强有力地支持该理论结果，且非凸模型中也观察到类似的隐私-审计紧张关系。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁在更现实的审计者威胁模型下审慎考虑隐私-审计权衡，为设计隐私保护的MU审计方案奠定基础。

Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

</details>


### [120] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过残差化控制发现：Qwen3-4B模型的代码正确性在生成前可从隐状态线性解码（AUC 0.931），但修复过程中的正确性信号实为修复上下文的统计相关，而非独立的修复理解特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型的隐状态中是否编码了代码正确性信息，特别是在代码生成前和修复失败代码的过程中。

Method: 在444个LiveCodeBench任务上，使用线性探针从Qwen3-4B-Instruct的隐状态解码代码正确性；通过残差化（residualization）消除提示长度等混淆变量的影响；在236个修复案例中检测隐状态偏移的对比方向并进行条件残差化验证。

Result: 生成前正确性线性可解码AUC为0.931，残差化后仍达0.911；修复过程中的对比方向在条件残差化后消失，表明其为修复上下文的伪相关。

Conclusion: 代码正确性在生成前确实编码在隐状态中，但修复成功信号主要来自修复上下文而非独立的修复理解机制；残差化是揭示此类混淆变量的有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [121] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了PCA在降维时可能保留超过99.9999%的方差却完全丢失罕见灾难性事件信号（称为"风险阴影"），并提出ExPCA和TP-PCA两种重新加权协方差以保留罕见事件信息的方法，理论上证明其优于传统PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA以方差最大化为目标，但方差保存与罕见高风险事件的信号保存之间存在根本性不匹配，可能导致在高风险决策场景中完全丢失关键信息。

Method: 提出Expectile PCA (ExPCA)和Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)两种方法，通过对数据协方差进行重新加权来增强对高影响事件的关注，并进行了理论分析和实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息方面严格优于PCA；在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 在高风险决策场景中，基于方差的降维方法需要根本性的重新思考，传统PCA存在严重局限性。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [122] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PS2-RL框架，通过两阶段架构（备份策略训练+可微投影层）实现在高维状态空间中可扩展的、具有形式化安全保障的强化学习，无需显式构造控制不变集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL方法存在两难：软约束方法无形式化安全保证，而具有严格保证的方法依赖显式证书函数构造控制不变集，该方法随状态维度增长扩展性差且行为过于保守。

Method: 两阶段架构：第一阶段通过安全到达价值函数训练备份策略，隐式在线生成控制不变集；第二阶段通过可微投影层对RL策略进行端到端训练，严格强制执行基于备份策略的安全保证。

Result: 在状态维度高达10的机器人控制任务上验证了方法的可扩展性和性能，优于此前难以扩展的严格安全RL方法。

Conclusion: PS2-RL框架在保证形式化安全的同时实现了良好的可扩展性和性能，且不限制底层RL算法，可即插即用。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [123] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可分解注意力偏置的Transformer神经算子，通过在注意力logits计算中引入空间局部性和旋转位置编码，实现零样本推理时对远大于训练时空间域的外推泛化，在多个PDE基准和3D工业大气流动任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子在复杂几何上的PDE求解中表现出色，但隐式假设固定域大小，缺乏对更大空间域的零样本泛化能力。

Method: 在注意力logits计算中引入可分解偏置来实现空间局部性，该偏置保持query-key内积的完全可分解性，兼容优化的注意力核；结合旋转位置编码，在不改变Transformer架构的前提下实现可控空间支持的表达性嵌入。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流动应用中，该方法显著提升了向更大域零样本泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 通过可分解的注意力局部性偏置结合旋转位置编码，能够在无需改变Transformer架构的条件下实现有效的域扩展泛化。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [124] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对消费级Ampere GPU上扩散Transformer的INT8量化后处理速度反而慢于FP8/NF4的问题，本文发现是由于当前实现仅将INT8量化后又立即反量化为bf16计算，未利用INT8张量核心。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核（int8×int8→int32，在Ampere张量核心上运行，支持per-token×per-channel反量化和bias融合）替换线性层中的反量化到bf16路径，实现了每个GEMM 2.8-4.2倍于bf16的加速，端到端在RTX 3090上768px加速约1.1倍，1024px单卡生成图像156.5秒，快于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），且质量无损。但该优势仅限于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上不及原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散Transformer的INT8 W8A8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际速度反而比FP8和NF4更慢，原因在于现有实现仅将权重和激活量化到INT8后立即反量化回bf16执行bf16矩阵乘法，从未真正调用GPU的INT8张量核心，导致硬件的计算优势完全未被利用。本文旨在消除这一软件伪影，释放INT8张量核心的真实加速潜力。

Method: 设计并实现一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核：在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32的整数矩阵乘法，在epilogue中融合per-token×per-channel反量化和bias加法，并按GEMM形状自动调优。将该内核直接替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中原有的反量化到bf16的计算路径。

Result: 该内核的int8×int8→int32累积计算与torch._int_mm逐位精确，反量化输出与参考输出的余弦相似度为1.0且无NaN。每个GEMM相比bf16加速2.8-4.2倍；端到端在RTX 3090上768px分辨率加速约1.1倍（~9-10%），1024px单卡生成图像156.5秒，快于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒）。速度上超越FP8约9.5%满足主要目标，超越NF4约4.9%但处于运行方差范围内。质量上PickScore/CLIPScore无显著差异。

Conclusion: INT8从最慢的变体变为最快的变体，使1024px分辨率在单GPU上可行。但该优势具有硬件特异性：仅适用于消费级Ampere GPU（如RTX 3090），在A100和B200上该内核反而输给这些卡的原生bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [125] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用一年高分辨率运行数据，通过统计与机器学习方法分析氢基多能源系统（H-MES），发现太阳能辐照度解释氢产量45.7%的秩方差，随机森林揭示风电的非线性预测重要性，并结合序列模型与强化学习实现运行预测与收益优化，证明统计与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对氢基多能源系统（H-MES）的运行特性缺乏基于高分辨率实际数据的深入统计表征，且统计方法与机器学习方法在此类系统建模与控制中的互补作用尚未被系统论证。

Method: 采用统计方法（秩方差分析、多元回归）与机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）相结合，对一年高分辨率运行数据进行分析与建模。

Result: 太阳能辐照度解释45.7%的氢产量秩方差（大效应量）；电力需求呈弱逆抑制效应（ε²=0.126）；电解槽功率为最优线性预测因子，存在太阳-风协同交互作用；随机森林将风电排在预测重要性首位（双变量相关仅r=0.167）；序列模型利用24小时自相关（r=0.845）实现运行预测；强化学习智能体优化了氢气收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计方法与机器学习方法在氢基多能源系统（H-MES）的建模与控制中具有互补性，两者结合能够更全面地揭示系统运行规律并优化控制策略。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [126] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对DiffusionGemma 26B模型的实证分析，发现其解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是存在部分从左到右的提交偏置，且这种偏置的强度完全依赖于分析粒度；模型以大并发批次提交token，批次内部顺序本质上是未定义的；行为具有任务依赖性。核心贡献是方法论上的：提出了测量解码顺序时必须处理的五个混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行的非自回归解码器，但现有工作几乎从未测量已发布检查点的实际token提交顺序。作者希望揭示扩散解码的真实行为，并建立严格的方法论来避免测量中的伪影。

Method: 在DiffusionGemma 26B（基于Gemma 4的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型）的采样器中插入钩子，记录每个画布位置提交的时机和置信度。使用686个提示、六种场景的探针套件，从token级别到块级别逐层粗化分析粒度。

Result: 1) 解码存在弱token级左到右偏置，随分析粒度粗化而平滑增强；"块大小"是测量尺度的伪影。2) 模型以大并发批次提交token，批次内顺序无定义。3) 行为具有任务依赖性：结构化JSON几乎以任意顺序提交；数学推理中提交置信度与正确性相关，事实回忆则无此信号。4) 提交过程在预算内短时间爆发式完成，任务准确率与自回归Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的实际解码行为远比"并行"或"块自回归"等标签复杂，其顺序特性在很大程度上是分析粒度的人为产物。方法论上，测量解码顺序必须处理尾部EOS填充、场景内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和提交批次内部平局等五个问题，否则会得到不存在的解码顺序结果。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [127] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导引扩散检测处理变量的跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的电导，并用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。其观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性滤波。理论分析将误差分解为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，给出有限样本界、潜在分段光滑几何下的图可容许性速率和有限路径选择校准。在54个合成基准单元中，有防护的观测A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF族在32个单元中击败最佳非A-IHF基线。当图捕获分段光滑一阶段结构时性能最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非仅一阶段预测。高容量一阶段模型可能插值处理变量，为结果方程留下过少残差信息。现有方法在处理高维或非线性一阶段模型时面临残差提取不足的问题，需要一种能够自适应提取有效残差的确定性方法。

Method: 提出A-IHF方法：将处理变量作为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导引扩散检测处理变量的较大跳跃，自适应地衰减这些跳跃处的图电导，然后使用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。观测选择规则仅使用工具变量(Z)和协变量(X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。理论框架将误差分解为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异三个成分。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元中，有防护的观测A-IHF取得最低平均结构响应MSE。A-IHF族在32个单元中击败最佳非A-IHF基线。在调优的图、核、树、Boosting、级数和神经网络控制函数基线中表现最佳。当图能够捕获分段光滑的一阶段结构时性能最为突出。

Conclusion: A-IHF作为一种新颖的确定性图扩散残差提取器，在控制函数设定中有效解决了高容量一阶段模型带来的残差信息不足问题，理论保证了有限样本性能，并在广泛的合成基准测试中超越多种现有方法。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [128] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中的探针反馈模型，证明即使少量含噪的成对比较探针（budget k ≤ T）也能显著降低最坏情况下的遗憾值，达到 O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)}) 的遗憾界，且该界在 T、k 和噪声参数 δ 上紧（对数因子除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两条近期研究线统一起来：一是专家设定中的亚线性最佳专家查询，二是在线凸优化中每轮可用的成对比较反馈。探索在标准全反馈 OCO 中引入有限预算的成对探针能否提升 regret 性能。

Method: 提出统一的成对探针模型（pairwise probing），允许学习者每轮选择两个点并查询哪个损失更小。结合方差缩减效应（variance reduction）和连续指数权重的二阶（基于方差）分析，对探针带来的提升进行量化。

Result: Regret_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，其中 k 为探针预算，δ 为噪声参数（δ=0 无噪声，δ=1/2 为随机猜测）。该界在 T、k、δ 上紧。在有限专家设定下，所得界在所有参数（包括 d）上完全紧。

Conclusion: 证明了即使亚线性且含噪的探针预算也能在最坏情况下显著提升全反馈 OCO 的 regret 性能；随着噪声接近抛硬币（δ→1/2），regret 保证平滑退化。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [129] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于有限字典和预算约束的不确定性集选择问题，提出了基于覆盖目标的原子不确定性集数据驱动选择方法，证明了该目标的单调性和子模性，支持贪心算法并具有(1-1/e)近似保证，同时提供了损失界限证明和半径校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习与优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择，现有方法缺乏对有限字典和预算约束下不确定性方向覆盖的系统性研究。

Method: 提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，覆盖评估方向（包括梯度、对抗扰动或留出数据上的偏移）；证明目标函数具有单调性和子模性，采用贪心方法并给出(1-1/e)近似保证及匹配的硬性下界；提供损失证书和半径校准规则。

Result: 贪心算法具有(1-1/e)近似保证，存在匹配的硬性下界；提供了所选子集损失的有界证书和具有样本外控制的半径校准规则。

Conclusion: 基于字典和预算约束的原子不确定性集选择问题可被高效求解，所提方法在理论保证和实用控制方面均有效。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [130] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型通用自适应策略框架，集成基于图的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和函数过程的泰勒近似，在亚线性时间和线性数据量内建立理论性能保证，并通过合成和真实交通数据集验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互联数据中的最优结构识别需要大量采样和计算资源，利用信号依赖关系可提升效率，但非线性统计关系的普遍性增加了复杂度，亟需开发能处理非线性依赖的高效自适应策略。

Method: 开发了基于图的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和函数过程泰勒近似相结合的通用自适应策略，在最小假设条件下建立亚线性时间和线性数据量的理论保证，并提出多种针对特定或扩展场景的修改方案。

Result: 建立了对噪声干扰、模型渐近收敛和解空间不匹配等多种不确定性的鲁棒性理论保证，通过合成基准和真实交通数据集数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架以极少的先验条件和假设实现了通用性，在理论和实践层面均证明了在处理非线性依赖的大规模数据最优结构识别问题中的有效性。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中编码的声学特征，发现没有单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间，组合嵌入效果最佳，并基于特征可恢复性与物种重要性提供了模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物声学领域广泛使用预训练音频嵌入，但对其编码的具体声学特征缺乏了解，限制了模型的透明度和向稀有物种或数据稀缺场景的推广。

Method: 使用88个eGeMAPS特征覆盖六个分类群，通过线性和非线性回归探针量化不同预训练模型对每种声学属性的编码能力，并通过交叉引用特征可恢复性与每物种特征显著性（NMI）进行分析。

Result: 无免费午餐模式成立，无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间；拼接嵌入表现最佳；响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），基频F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过交叉分析可恢复性与物种特征显著性，可推导出数据驱动的模型选择策略，为生物声学任务提供实用性指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [132] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于路由的双适配器知识编辑方法，通过分离编辑注入和旁路抑制来提升模型编辑的准确性和局部保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要更新选定事实，同时保持邻近但无关行为不受影响。核心设计问题不仅在于如何写入编辑，还在于何时抑制编辑。现有方法在编辑准确性与局部保持性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出\method{}方法，包含一个相关性路由器（决定是否接收编辑记忆）和两个适配器：编辑适配器（使路由到的提示偏好新对象）和局部适配器（使未路由的直接提示保持或恢复原对象偏好）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，在三个基准（CF、ZSRE、MQuAKE）上分别取得最佳总体概率偏好准确率：0.8180、0.8946、0.9922；在Qwen3-8B上也有相同趋势。路由消融实验表明不同数据集的最佳路由策略不同（CF上词法神经路由器最优，ZSRE和MQuAKE上BGE嵌入路由更优）。组件消融表明性能提升主要来自分离编辑注入与旁路抑制，而非简单增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 知识编辑系统的关键在于区分何时应用编辑、何时保持原知识，双适配器加路由器的分离设计能有效兼顾编辑准确性与局部保持性，且不同数据分布需要匹配不同的路由策略。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [133] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Braun等人提出的压缩计算(CC)玩具模型是否属于叠加计算实例，发现CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，性能提升源于标签中的非预期混合矩阵，而非真正的叠加计算，因此CC模型不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证CC玩具模型是否真正实现了叠加计算（即用更少的神经元计算更多函数），从而判断其是否可以作为叠加计算研究的合适的玩具模型。

Method: 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分析混合矩阵对性能的影响；通过特征值分解分析神经元方向与混合矩阵的关系；构建基于混合矩阵的半非负矩阵分解(SNMF)基线进行比较。

Result: 性能提升与混合矩阵的幅度呈正相关，移除混合矩阵后性能消失；学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；SNMF基线可复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但未达到训练模型性能。

Conclusion: CC模型的表现主要受混合矩阵支配，并非真正的叠加计算，因此不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于任意有界凸容量集上的在线库存优化问题，维护在线学习器选择的隐藏目标并投影到当前可行集的简单原则是最优的。基于在线梯度下降的方法将遗憾界从逆概率依赖改进为平方根逆概率依赖，并给出了强凸损失下的首个多对数遗憾界以及动态遗憾界。分析引入范数对齐原则，将问题降维为一维队列控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化中，库存结转使得可行动作集依赖于历史决策，增加了问题复杂性。已有方法仅适用于单一线型容量约束，缺乏对一般凸容量集的理论保证，且遗憾界依赖于需求概率的逆（而非平方根逆），收敛速度不理想。

Method: 提出隐藏目标投影原则：维护在线学习器选择的隐藏目标，将其投影到当前可行的补货集上执行。以在线梯度下降为基础学习器，引入范数对齐原则，将隐藏目标到可行集的距离（以投影所用同一范数度量）建模为标量队列演化过程，其中目标移动为到达过程，共同需求为服务过程。

Result: 1) 将一般凸集上的遗憾界从O(1/√(p))改进为O(1/√p)（p为共同需求概率），并证明匹配下界；2) 给出强凸损失下的首个多对数遗憾界；3) 给出适应欧几里得路径变化的动态遗憾界；4) 合成与真实库存数据实验验证理论。

Conclusion: 隐藏目标投影原则在任意有界凸容量集上是最优的，范数对齐方法将高维状态依赖降为一维队列控制，解决了先前逐产品方法无法处理的通用凸容量集问题。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [135] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多组均值估计d臂赌博机中的最大风险主动学习目标，提出了局部极小极大框架，推导了首个普适下界，并引入了方差局部曲率（VLC）概念刻画假设类复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习理论缺乏针对多组均值估计中最大风险（worst-case uncertainty）目标的系统性下界分析，尤其未能将难度分解为预算、异方差性和模型复杂度三个正交因素。

Method: 建立局部极小极大框架，提出损失诱导的ℓ1决策空间几何与基于表示法的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算，推导VLC复杂度度量。

Result: 证明了首个适用于任意有限方差假设类的下界，下界由预算项、异方差性指标和VLC三项构成；VLC在光滑类中可重参数化为方差-费希尔信息量，常见分布族有闭式解；与最强上界对比表明在广泛场景下近最优（对数因子内），但在高异质性实例中存在系统性差距。

Conclusion: 本文为多组均值估计的最大风险主动学习提供了理论基础，揭示了问题难度的正交分解结构，并为未来缩小上下界差距指明了方向。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度策略，仅利用加工时间矩阵作为输入，在小规模Taillard基准（4×4至10×10）上训练，能够零迁移泛化至40×40至100×100的大规模实例，makespan平均间隙为12.89%-15.12%，性能优于SPT/LPT，接近EST，为经典调度规则提供了轻量级学习替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放车间调度问题（OSSP）在工业和服务场景中广泛存在，但精确方法在大规模时计算不可行，经典调度规则和元启发式方法需要大量调参才能维持解质量。本文旨在开发一种无需特征工程、可从小规模推广至大规模问题的学习型调度策略。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，结合多头注意力机制；输入仅为加工时间矩阵；在Taillard基准的小规模实例（4×4, 5×5, 7×7, 10×10）上训练；训练后的策略直接用于大规模随机生成实例（40×40至100×100），无需重新训练或微调。

Result: 在小规模实例上，生成的可行调度makespan在最优已知值的15%-30%以内；在大规模实例上，与标准下界相比平均间隙为12.89%-15.12%；与EST规则相比差距较小，显著优于SPT和LPT规则。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的策略可以在小规模OSSP实例上训练后泛化至更大规模问题，无需特征工程即可提供一种有竞争力的学习型调度方法，可作为经典调度规则的有效替代方案。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [137] [UP-NRPA: User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation for Planning with Large Language Models in Goal-oriented Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13683)
*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UP-NRPA在线框架，利用大语言模型和用户画像实现对话策略的动态自适应，无需离线强化学习训练，在协作与非协作对话基准测试中取得显著效果，谈判任务销售率提升56.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话策略规划方法难以动态适应多样化用户特征，依赖模型训练和离线强化学习策略模型，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA)在线框架，利用大语言模型，通过实时用户反馈及从当前用户画像映射出的个性、偏好和目标，实现对话策略的动态自适应，无需离线强化学习。

Result: 在协作与非协作对话基准测试中表现优异，多个对话任务实现100%成功率；谈判任务中销售率(SL)提升56.41%。

Conclusion: UP-NRPA无需训练机制即可适应多样化用户需求，使对话系统能够动态适配用户特征。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>


### [138] [History of the Muddy Children Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13703)
*Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯了“泥巴孩子谜题”在过去两个世纪中在逻辑和文学出版物中的起源，并介绍了其众多变体（如数字或彩色帽子谜题），同时提出了一个涉及自指的新型帽子谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清“泥巴孩子谜题”的真正起源，因为该谜题对认知逻辑的发展具有重要启发意义，但其最初提出者尚不明确。

Method: 通过回溯近两个世纪的逻辑与文学出版物，追溯谜题的历史渊源；并在此基础上提出一个涉及自指的新帽子谜题。

Result: 梳理了“泥巴孩子谜题”的起源脉络，发现了众多变体（数字变体、彩色帽子变体等），并原创性地提出了一个自指型帽子谜题。

Conclusion: 泥巴孩子谜题的真正起源仍不明确，但该谜题及其变体在认知逻辑领域具有持续的启发价值，自指型新谜题进一步拓展了该方向。

Abstract: The Muddy Children Puzzle is a puzzle about knowledge and ignorance that has been inspiring for the development of epistemic logic. Who came up with it first? This is unclear. We trace the origin of the Muddy Children Puzzle through logical and literary publications over the past two centuries. The puzzle inspired a numerous variations such as involving numbers or coloured hats. We also present a novel hats puzzle involving self-reference.

</details>


### [139] [Orchestra-o1: Omnimodal Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13707)
*Fan Zhang,Vireo Zhang,Shengju Qian,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Donghao Zhou,Zhihong Zhu,Zheng Lian,Xin Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Orchestra-o1，一个面向全模态场景的智能体编排框架，支持模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行，在 OmniGAIA 基准上超越第二名 10.3% 的准确率，并通过 DA-GRPO 强化学习方法训练出性能领先的开源全模态智能体 Orchestra-o1-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编排框架局限于少数模态，难以泛化到异构模态共存与交互的复杂全模态场景，亟需一种能够统一理解与协调文本、图像、音频、视频等多种输入的编排机制。

Method: 提出 Orchestra-o1 全模态智能体编排框架，包含统一编排机制、模态感知任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行；并引入决策对齐组相对策略优化（DA-GRPO）用于高效智能体强化学习训练。

Result: 在 OmniGAIA 基准上超越第二名方法 10.3% 准确率；Orchestra-o1-8B 在所有现有开源全模态智能体中达到最优性能。

Conclusion: Orchestra-o1 通过统一编排与模态感知设计有效解决了全模态场景下的异构信息协调难题，DA-GRPO 进一步提升了训练效率与模型性能。

Abstract: The recent success of agent swarms has shifted the paradigm of large language model (LLM)-based agents from single-agent workflows to multi-agent systems, highlighting the importance of agent orchestration for task decomposition and collaboration. However, existing orchestration frameworks are limited to a narrow set of modalities and struggle to generalize to more complex settings where heterogeneous modalities coexist and interact. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in omnimodal scenarios, where tasks require the unified understanding and coordination of diverse inputs such as text, image, audio, and video. In this work, we propose Orchestra-o1, an omnimodal agent orchestration framework designed to support efficient agent collaboration across multiple modalities. Orchestra-o1 introduces a unified orchestration mechanism that enables modality-aware task decomposition, online sub-agent specialization, and parallel sub-task execution. This scalable design allows agent systems to effectively tackle complex real-world tasks involving heterogeneous information sources, surpassing the second-best approach by 10.3% accuracy on the OmniGAIA benchmark. Furthermore, we introduce decision-aligned group relative policy optimization (DA-GRPO), an efficient agentic reinforcement learning approach for training Orchestra-o1-8B, which also achieves state-of-the-art performance against all existing open-source omnimodal agents.

</details>


### [140] [Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710)
*Hongming Piao,Chi Liu,Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Ying Wei,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HOTE框架，通过混合模式强化学习实现提议者、求解者和评判者的协同进化，使8B模型在深度研究基准上超越8-32B静态模型，弥合了深度研究与智能体进化的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究任务受限于静态参数化能力，而智能体进化仅在可验证任务上有效，二者之间存在差距，缺乏针对开放研究任务的进化机制。

Method: 提出混合开放端三元进化（HOTE）框架，基于Web规模知识，通过混合模式强化学习促进提议者（Proposer）、求解者（Solver）和评判者（Judge）三个模块的协同进化。

Result: 在三个长文本深度研究基准上，8B模型超过最强静态开放8-32B模型及现有深度研究训练方法，且时间开销更低；三个模块的进化缺一不可。

Conclusion: HOTE框架有效弥合了静态深度研究与进化智能体之间的差距，为开放端任务中的自主进化智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep research and agent evolution serve as de-facto tasks for AI agents in real-world applications toward artificial general intelligence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, its effectiveness has been widely validated only on verifiable tasks with standard answers, leaving a gap with open-ended research tasks. To bridge these two critical tasks, we propose the Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution (HOTE) framework, which leverages hybrid-mode reinforcement learning to facilitate the collaborative evolution of a proposer, solver and judge based on web-scale knowledge, moving toward autonomous evolving agents in open-ended tasks and environments. Extensive experiments on three long-form deep research benchmarks demonstrate that the 8B model trained via HOTE surpasses the strongest static open 8-32B models as well as those trained by state-of-the-art deep research training methods with less time overhead, and further verify that the evolution of all three modules in HOTE is indispensable.

</details>


### [141] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkBench基准测试2024-2026年进展：最佳AI代理任务完成率从43%提升至89%，意外有害行为从26%降至2.5%。能力与安全性正相关而非权衡；前沿模型虽消除多类错误但仍存在基本失误；开源模型大幅降低高性能门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估和追踪前沿AI代理在任务完成能力与安全性方面的进展，特别是验证能力与安全性是否存在权衡关系。

Method: 对比2024年3月（GPT-4）与2026年6月（Claude Opus 4.8）在WorkBench基准上的表现，分析任务完成率与意外有害行为发生率，同时比较开源模型与专有模型的性能成本。

Result: 最佳代理任务完成率从43%提升至89%，意外有害行为从26%降至2.5%；能力与安全性正相关；多类错误已被消除但仍有基本失误；开源模型大幅降低了高性能水平的访问成本。

Conclusion: 前沿AI代理在能力和安全性上均取得显著进步，且两者呈正相关而非权衡关系；但仍需关注基本错误带来的不可逆风险；开源模型的崛起正在改变性能获取的成本结构。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>


### [142] [Refusal Beyond a Single Direction: A Preliminary Comparison of Diff-in-Means and INLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13720)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 比较DiM和INLP两种干预方法在五个开源聊天模型中控制拒绝行为的效果，发现INLP反事实翻转可与DiM方向消融相媲美，而零空间投影效果较弱；限制INLP到子空间主导方向可在保持近基线困惑度的同时实现可调控制；两种INLP干预在激活空间中产生性质不同的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索INLP方法能否匹配DiM在引导模型拒绝行为上的效果，以及INLP更丰富的参数化是否能产生更可调节的干预。

Method: 在五个开源聊天模型上，比较DiM的两种干预（激活添加和方向消融）与INLP的两种干预（零空间投影和反事实翻转），并分析限制INLP到子空间主导方向的效果。

Result: INLP反事实翻转在抑制拒绝行为上与DiM方向消融效果相当，零空间投影始终较弱；限制INLP到主导方向可在接近基线困惑度下保留大部分抑制效果；两种INLP干预在几何上产生性质不同的激活空间区域。

Conclusion: INLP的反事实翻转和DiM的方向消融都是有效的拒绝控制方法，限制INLP可提供可调能力；模型编码概念的缺失与编码其对立面在几何上是不同的，这值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Arditi et al. (2024) has shown that refusal in safety fine-tuned chat models is mediated by a single linear direction in the residual stream, recoverable by a difference-in-means (DiM) of harmful and harmless activations. We compare DiM-based interventions (activation addition and directional ablation) with two interventions derived from Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP) -- nullspace projection and counterfactual flipping -- on five open-weight chat models, asking whether INLP can match DiM at steering refusal and whether its richer parameterisation yields more tweakable interventions. INLP counterfactual flipping is competitive with DiM directional ablation on refusal suppression, while nullspace projection is consistently weaker. Restricting INLP to the leading directions of the extracted subspace preserves most of the suppression effect at near-baseline perplexity, giving a tunable capability. Geometrically, the two INLP interventions land in qualitatively different regions of activation space: nullspace projection collapses transformed activations \emph{between} the harmful and harmless clusters, while counterfactual flipping moves them into the opposite cluster, suggesting that the model encodes the absence of a concept differently from its opposite -- an intriguing distinction that warrants further investigation in future work.

</details>


### [143] [YeasierAgent: Agentic Social Sandbox as a Canvas for Intent-Driven Creation of Platform-Agnostic Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13722)
*Jory He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YeasierAgent 提出了一种基于共生代理、叙事世界和场景感知交互的应用构建范式，将应用重新定义为用户、代理和世界之间的协作空间，实现了跨平台的代理原生应用快速构建，并统一了智能代理的情感陪伴与实用工具执行功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统的设备耦合软件模型，解决现有应用开发中固定图形布局的局限性，以及智能代理在情感陪伴和工具执行功能上的割裂问题。

Method: 提出平台无关的交互单元（代理、场景、对话）替代固定图形布局，通过自动化生成、用户创建世界和空间多代理协作，构建共生代理原生应用范式。

Result: 实现了跨平台的代理原生应用快速构建，统一了情感陪伴与实用工具执行功能，形式化了共生代理原生应用类别，展示了从孤立的工具型聊天机器人向 cohesive、社会嵌入的计算环境的转变。

Conclusion: YeasierAgent 标志着从孤立的、工具特定的聊天机器人向 cohesive、社会嵌入的计算环境的范式转变，为代理原生应用领域提供了新的形式化框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.

</details>


### [144] [TwinBI: An Agentic Digital Twin for Efficient Augmented Interactions with Business Intelligence Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13731)
*Jisoo Jang Wen-Syan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinBI提出了一种智能体数字孪生框架，将LLM代理系统与可执行的BI仪表盘状态耦合，通过统一交互日志重建共享分析状态，从而在仪表盘交互和自然语言查询之间保持分析状态一致性。实验表明，该方法在精确匹配准确率（43.3%→63.3%）、部分匹配准确率（48.3%→70.8%）上显著提升，并将超时率从40.0%降至10.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业智能（BI）系统越来越多地结合仪表盘交互与基于LLM的助手，但用户在直接操作仪表盘和使用自然语言查询之间切换时，两种模式在多步分析过程中常出现状态不同步问题，导致跨过滤器、层级、指标和图表上下文难以保持一致的分析状态。

Method: TwinBI是一个智能体数字孪生框架，核心思路是将LLM代理系统与可执行的BI仪表盘状态进行耦合。通过统一的交互日志重建共享分析状态，从而统一对话交互、仪表盘操作、语义锚定和溯源追踪，并提供模式视图、SQL、日志及/insights命令等分析工件。

Result: 在A/B基准测试中，TwinBI将精确匹配准确率从43.3%提升至63.3%，部分匹配准确率从48.3%提升至70.8%，超时率从40.0%大幅降至10.0%。可用性研究表明，集成仪表盘与聊天的工作流有助于提升任务准确率和用户满意度。

Conclusion: TwinBI通过将可见的仪表盘状态转化为更丰富的可操作上下文，同时提升了智能体层面的分析可靠性和用户层面的分析支持能力。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) increasingly combines dashboard interaction with LLM-based assistance, but these two modes often fall out of sync during multi-step analysis. As users switch between direct dashboard manipulation and natural-language queries, it becomes difficult to preserve a consistent analytical state across filters, hierarchies, metrics, and chart context. We present TwinBI, an agentic digital-twin framework that couples an LLM-based agent system with an executable BI dashboard state. TwinBI unifies conversational interaction, dashboard manipulation, semantic grounding, and provenance tracking through a shared analytical state reconstructed from a unified interaction log. It also exposes artifacts such as schema views, SQL, logs, and an /insights command for state-grounded analytical summaries. We evaluate TwinBI in two complementary ways. In a controlled A/B benchmark with the same backbone agent, TwinBI improves exact-match accuracy from 43.3% to 63.3%, partial-credit accuracy from 48.3% to 70.8%, and substantially reduces timeout rate from 40.0% to 10.0% relative to Dashboard alone. In a usability study, participants benefited from the integrated dashboard-and-chat workflow, with high task accuracy, moderate workload, and favorable ratings for state-aware interaction mechanisms. These results suggest that TwinBI improves both agent-level analytical reliability and user-facing analytical support by turning visible dashboard state into richer actionable context. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/simonjisu/TwinBI

</details>


### [145] [When Sample Selection Bias Precipitates Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13732)
*Xinbao Qiao,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Peihua Mai,Meng Zhang,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在低资源数据孤岛场景下，基于局部参考分布的数据选择不仅不能防止模型崩溃，反而会加速崩溃并导致幂律多样性衰减；提出通过构建多孤岛间的Wasserstein代理参考分布来缓解此问题，而无需共享原始数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归训练合成数据可缓解数据稀缺但存在模型崩溃风险，数据选择被视作补救措施，但其可靠性关键取决于验证器使用的参考分布。在低资源验证场景下，每个验证器只能观察到目标流形的小型、碎片化且有偏的切片，选择本身会引入偏差。

Method: 理论上证明了数据孤岛式选择会加速模型崩溃并诱导幂律多样性衰减；提出在不共享原始数据的前提下，从多个孤岛构建Wasserstein代理参考分布作为缓解方案。

Result: 实验证实基于局部参考分布的选择在偏斜分布上失败，而协作式代理参考分布能够缓解多样性退化。

Conclusion: 递归合成数据管道在真实数据覆盖碎片化或稀缺时需要特别谨慎，跨孤岛的协作式参考分布是一种有效的初步缓解手段。

Abstract: The proliferation of recursive training on synthetic data can alleviate data scarcity but risks model collapse, where repeated training erodes distributional tails and homogenizes outputs. Data selection is widely viewed as a remedy, yet its reliability depends critically on the reference distribution used by the verifier. We show that in low-resource verification regimes, where each verifier observes only a small, fragmented, and biased slice of the target manifold, selection itself becomes biased. This situation naturally arises in low-resource data silos such as healthcare consortia or proprietary financial institutions, where raw data cannot be pooled and local references are inherently incomplete. As a result, selection preferentially retains samples aligned with the local manifold while pruning globally relevant tail modes, turning from a safeguard against collapse into a mechanism that precipitates it. We theoretically prove that such siloed selection accelerates collapse and induces power-law diversity decay. As an initial mitigation, we construct Wasserstein proxy references from multiple silos without sharing raw data. Empirical results confirm that local-reference selection fails on skewed distributions, whereas collaborative proxy references mitigate diversity degradation, suggesting that recursive synthetic-data pipelines require particular caution when real-data coverage is fragmented or scarce.

</details>


### [146] [AI Receptivity or AI Adoption Breadth? A Tool-Specific Reanalysis of the Lower-Literacy/Higher-Usage Link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13734)
*Hristo Inouzhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究复现并扩展了Tully等人(2025)关于AI素养与AI接受度关系的分析，发现总体上的负向关联主要源于非文本AI工具的使用差异，而文本AI工具的使用与AI素养无显著关联，且该关系主要体现在采用/未采用层面而非使用强度层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验Tully等人(2025)提出的"较低AI素养预测较高AI接受度"这一主张，验证其结论的稳健性并探究聚合层面效应背后的异质性来源。

Method: 使用Tully等人(2025)研究3的公开数据，采用OLS、二元Logit、有序Logit和多项Logit等多种规格进行复现分析，并按工具类型（文本AI与非文本AI）进行异质性分析，同时调整人口统计学协变量。

Result: 聚合层面复制了AI素养与AI使用之间的负向关联，但发现该效应存在显著的异质性：AI素养对文本AI使用无显著预测作用（β=-0.090, p=.387），而对非文本AI使用有强预测作用（β=-0.377, p<.001），且该效应主要表现为采用/非采用模式（OR=0.68）而非使用强度差异。

Conclusion: 不支持"较低AI素养普遍预测较高AI接受度"的简单论断，而是指向一个更狭窄的模式：较低AI素养与较低渗透率的非文本AI工具的广泛采用相关。

Abstract: Recent evidence reported by Tully, Longoni, and Appel (2025) suggests that lower artificial intelligence (AI) literacy predicts greater receptivity toward AI. We revisit this claim using the public data from Study 3 of that article, which measures past usage of five AI tool categories on a five-point frequency scale. We first reproduce the negative association between AI literacy and aggregate AI usage using OLS on participant-level averages, binary logit, ordered logit, and multinomial logit specifications. We then show that the aggregate relationship masks substantial heterogeneity by tool type. In our demographic-adjusted primary specification, AI literacy does not significantly predict text AI usage (ordered-logit $β$ = -0.090, p = .387), whereas it remains a strong predictor of non-text AI adoption ($β$ = -0.377, p < .001). The non-text effect is also robust under Tully et al.'s original Study 3 control specification ($β$ = -0.502, p < .001). Binary, ordered-logit, and multinomial specifications suggest that the non-text relationship is primarily an adoption/non-adoption pattern rather than evidence of intensive use: the demographic-adjusted odds ratio of ever having used a non-text AI tool is 0.68. Thus, in the study that measures self-reported past usage rather than stated preferences, the evidence does not support a simple claim that lower AI literacy predicts greater receptivity to AI in general. It points instead to a narrower pattern of broader adoption across lower-penetration, non-text AI tools.

</details>


### [147] [MA-ProofBench: A Two-Tiered Evaluation of LLMs for Theorem Proving in Mathematical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13782)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-ProofBench，据我们所知是首个专注于数学分析的定理证明基准。基准包含200个形式化定理，涵盖6个核心主题和27个子类别，分为本科（Level I）和博士资格考试（Level II）两个难度级别。当前最先进的模型GPT-5.5在Level I上仅达到16% Pass@8，在Level II上仅达到5%，多数模型在Level II上接近0%。主要失败模式为Mathlib幻觉和不完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明基准在数学覆盖范围和难度上存在局限，主要集中在代数、初等数论等易于形式化的领域，而缺乏对需要深层推理的数学分析子领域的覆盖。为弥补这一空白，作者提出了专门面向数学分析的基准MA-ProofBench。

Method: 构建了包含200个形式化定理的基准，涵盖测度与积分理论、复分析、泛函分析等6个核心主题和27个子类别，分为Level I（本科）和Level II（博士资格考试）两个难度级别。每个问题通过人工主导、LLM辅助的形式化流程构建，并经过独立专家评审。评估了多个通用推理模型和形式定理证明器。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.5在Level I上仅达16% Pass@8，Level II上为5%；多数模型在Level II上接近0%。主要失败模式为Mathlib幻觉（生成不存在的形式化定义或引理）和不完整证明。在自然语言版本上的评估揭示非形式推理与形式推理之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: MA-ProofBench可作为跟踪高级领域形式数学推理进展的可靠参考基准，当前LLM在数学分析的形式化证明方面表现仍然较弱，有待进一步提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made notable progress in automated theorem proving, yet existing formal benchmarks remain limited in both mathematical coverage and difficulty. Most are concentrated in areas that are easier to formalize, such as algebra and elementary number theory, and provide limited coverage of subfields that require deeper reasoning, including mathematical analysis. To address this gap, we introduce MA-ProofBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal theorem-proving benchmark dedicated to Mathematical Analysis. The benchmark contains 200 formalized theorems covering 6 core topics and 27 subcategories, including measure and integration theory, complex analysis, and functional analysis. The problems are divided into two difficulty levels, an undergraduate level (Level I, 100 problems) and a Ph.D. qualifying level (Level II, 100 problems), to evaluate how well LLMs perform formal reasoning at different mathematical depths. Each problem is constructed through a human-led, LLM-assisted formalization pipeline followed by independent expert review, ensuring that the formal statements remain faithful to the original mathematics. We evaluate a range of recent general-purpose reasoning models and formal theorem provers on MA-ProofBench. However, most models perform poorly: even the best-performing model, GPT-5.5, achieves only 16% Pass@8 on Level I and 5% on Level II, while most models stay close to 0% on Level II. Further analysis identifies Mathlib hallucinations and incomplete proofs as the two dominant failure modes, while an evaluation on the natural-language version of the benchmark exposes a clear gap between informal and formal reasoning. MA-ProofBench is intended to serve as a reliable reference for tracking progress in formal mathematical reasoning in advanced domains.

</details>


### [148] [Poker Arena: Multi-Axis Profiling of Strategic Reasoning and Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13815)
*Pratham Singla,Shivank Garg,Vihan Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Poker Arena平台，通过九维认知画像分解策略推理能力，发现多维度评估揭示出标量排行榜系统性地错误排序LLM能力结构，跨维度一致性比单一维度峰值表现更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈基准将异构推理维度压缩为单一标量，未能揭示前沿LLM的能力结构，因此需要更细粒度的多维评估框架。

Method: 构建Poker Arena无限注德州扑克锦标赛平台，集成三层记忆架构（局内、会话、跨会话）与九轴认知画像（如下注尺度校准、位置意识等），对7个前沿模型进行50轮每轮1000手的评估及记忆消融实验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6赢得+15,730筹码和14次第一名，但在平均轴得分上仅排名第五（共7个模型）；持续记忆对某些模型有帮助，对另一些模型则有损害；锦标赛筹码排名与平均轴得分排名不一致。

Conclusion: 多轴评估揭示了标量排行榜系统性地误判能力结构，跨维度一致性比单一维度峰值表现更重要。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning under uncertainty underpins consequential decisions in negotiation, finance, and policy, but prevailing game-play benchmarks collapse heterogeneous reasoning dimensions into a single scalar, leaving the capability structure of frontier LLMs unexamined. We introduce Poker Arena, a no-limit Texas Hold'em tournament platform that couples a three-layer memory architecture (within-hand, session, and cross-session) with a nine-axis cognitive profile decomposing strategic reasoning into interpretable dimensions such as bet-sizing calibration and positional awareness. We evaluate seven frontier models across 50 sessions of 1,000 hands and a controlled memory ablation; tournament chips and aggregate axis score order the field differently: Claude Opus 4.6 wins +$15,730 chips with 14 first-place finishes, yet ranks only fifth of seven on mean axis score, while persistent memory helps some models and hurts others. These findings show that multi-axis evaluation surfaces capability structure that scalar leaderboards systematically misrank, with cross-dimensional consistency outweighing peak performance on any single axis.

</details>


### [149] [Hyperdimensional computing for structured querying on tabular data embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13871)
*Sebastián Bugedo,Stijn Vansummeren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用超维计算（HDC）中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入，利用其代数性质推导出可解释的相似度期望值，从而在向量空间中实现结构化查询的检索与零匹配检测，在多个数据集上优于或持平图基线方法EmbDI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据嵌入方法（如行、列、表嵌入）存在相似度分数不可解释的根本局限：查询与其最近邻之间的具体相似度值没有内在语义，无法判断该邻接项是真实匹配还是数据集中无有效答案时"最不相似"的项。这种无法设置原则性阈值的问题阻碍了实际部署，特别是零匹配检测场景。

Method: 采用超维计算（HDC）中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入。利用HDC操作的代数性质（如捆绑、绑定等），推导出等值和非等值检索谓词下期望相似度值的闭式解，这些值随维度增加收敛到可解释的数值，并据此确定合适的检索阈值。

Result: 在两种真实数据集上，HDC在所有配置下匹配或优于EmbDI的行检索性能；更鲁棒地处理非等值谓词；在足够维度下实现完美的属性投影精度；且通过其原则性阈值唯一地实现了零匹配谓词的可靠识别。

Conclusion: HDC/HRR框架能够为表格行嵌入提供可解释的相似度分数和原则性的检索阈值，解决了现有嵌入方法在零匹配检测方面的根本缺陷，在行检索任务上达到或超越现有基线方法。

Abstract: Tabular data embeddings have become a cornerstone of data profiling and data integration pipelines, enabling tasks such as entity annotation and resolution; schema matching; column type detection; and table search, among others. Existing approaches embed rows, columns, or entire tables into a vector space and rely on nearest-neighbor search to retrieve candidate matches. A fundamental limitation of current embedding methods is the lack of interpretable similarity scores: the concrete similarity value between a query and its nearest neighbour carries no intrinsic meaning, making it impossible to determine whether that neighbour is a true match or simply the least-dissimilar item in a corpus that contains no valid answer. This inability to set principled thresholds for retrieval undermines practical deployment, particularly for zero-match detection.
  We investigate the use of HyperDimensional Computing (HDC), specifically the Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) model, as a framework for tabular row embeddings when the retrieval task corresponds to answering structured select-project queries in vector space. Exploiting the algebraic properties of HDC operations, we derive closed-form expected similarity values for both equality and non-equality retrieval predicates, which converge to interpretable values as dimensionality increases, and use these to identify suitable retrieval thresholds. We evaluate HDC against EmbDI, a graph-based baseline, on two real-world datasets across varying table sizes and predicate lengths. Our results show that HDC matches or outperforms EmbDI for row retrieval across all configurations, handles non-equality predicates more robustly, and achieves perfect attribute projection accuracy at sufficient dimensionality -- while uniquely enabling reliable identification of zero-match predicates through its principled thresholds.

</details>


### [150] [Capability Minimization as a Safety Primitive: Risk-Aware Causal Gating for Least-Privilege LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13884)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）框架，通过结合因果效应估计与校准风险控制，决定是否执行、延迟或放弃模型预测，在高风险场景下显著降低高成本错误，优于基于置信度的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代决策系统中学习组件的输出可能自信但错误，导致下游行动面临高成本错误。现有基于预测置信度的方法无法区分因果风险与预测不确定性，缺乏可靠的安全约束机制。

Method: 提出RACG框架：1）建模从候选行动到结果的因果路径；2）基于估计的反事实风险而非原始预测置信度进行门控决策；3）推导无分布假设的高风险行动概率边界，转化为满足用户指定安全约束的操作阈值；4）提出自适应门控策略，通过监控预测与实际结果的差异来应对分布偏移。

Result: 在模拟干预和真实决策基准中，RACG显著减少高成本错误，同时保留无门控策略的大部分效用；在匹配的弃权率下，优于基于置信度和选择性预测的基线方法。

Conclusion: 明确分离因果风险与预测不确定性能够构建更安全、更透明的决策系统，为高风险场景下的可信自动化提供原则性机制。
